Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the chemical structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 511. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 853 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_511(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 511. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^853(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Statement: For any chemical module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 141. The projective resolution has length at most 479 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

For the inductive step, we decompose the chemical structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 479. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
